- **spectral-clustering-of-generated-expression-profiles**: Use when you have GAN-generated single-cell expression profiles produced by mapping spatial transcriptomics data (x1) into the scRNA-seq domain, and you need to verify or derive spatially informed structure at single-cell resolution. - **spectral-cosine-similarity-evaluation**: Use when you have predicted MS/MS spectra (e.g. MGF from 3DMolMS pred_msms) paired with experimental test spectra (PKL/MGF) and need per-compound cosine similarity scores stratified by ion mode or collision energy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-data-visualization) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-database-compilation**: Use when you have generated predicted fragment spectra for a set of compounds using CFM-ID or similar in-silico prediction tools and need to organize these results into a persistent, queryable database that mirrors the schema of an existing online spectral reference (such as the predicted-fragments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-database-compilation) · 2 copies)
- **spectral-database-dereplication**: Use when untargeted LC-MS/MS data in .mzML format needs spectral library dereplication against GNPS/HMDB/MassBank as the first annotation stage of MAW, before in silico tools like SIRIUS or MetFrag. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-database-dereplication--464b3c0140a07401))
- **spectral-database-dereplication**: Use when you have centroided LC-MS/MS spectra (MGF/mzXML/mzML/mzData) and want to identify known peptidic natural products or other metabolite classes by database search with FDR-controlled matches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-database-dereplication--5e746d2d150f60f7))
- **spectral-database-dereplication-ms2**: Use when processing MS2 .mzML metabolomics data and you need precursor-anchored spectral matches to GNPS, HMDB, and MassBank to assign MSI-level 2 annotations (e.g., bryophyte features like M287R318/Nicotiflorin). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/spectral-database-dereplication-ms2))
- **spectral-database-integration-and-sampling**: Use when you need to generate synthetic LC/GC-MS feature tables or raw mzML files with realistic peak complexity, ion multiplicities, and natural spectral variation—not just theoretical m/z values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/spectral-database-integration-and-sampling) · 3 copies)
